Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the general structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 203. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 158 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to general structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 203/158 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The general variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 858 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 420 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to general theory, we decompose the problem into 858 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 420. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
